SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD = 0.55
FRAME_DIFFERENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.15

_clahe = cv2.createCLAHE(clipLimit=2.5, tileGridSize=(8, 8))

def enhance_frame_for_cctv(frame):
    try:
        lab = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2LAB)
        l, a, b = cv2.split(lab)
        l_enhanced = _clahe.apply(l)
        enhanced = cv2.cvtColor(cv2.merge([l_enhanced, a, b]), cv2.COLOR_LAB2BGR)
        return cv2.bilateralFilter(enhanced, 5, 40, 40)
    except:
        return frame

def enhance_face_crop(face_image):
    if face_image is None or face_image.size == 0:
        return face_image
    try:
        lab = cv2.cvtColor(face_image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2LAB)
        l, a, b = cv2.split(lab)
        l_enhanced = _clahe.apply(l)
        return cv2.cvtColor(cv2.merge([l_enhanced, a, b]), cv2.COLOR_LAB2BGR)
    except:
        return face_image

def calculate_frame_difference(frame1, frame2):
    if frame1 is None or frame2 is None:
        return 1.0
    gray1 = cv2.cvtColor(frame1, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    gray2 = cv2.cvtColor(frame2, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    diff = cv2.absdiff(gray1, gray2)
    diff_score = np.sum(diff) / (gray1.shape[0] * gray1.shape[1] * 255)
    return diff_score

def extract_intelligent_frames(video_path, max_frames=10):
    print(f"Processing video: {video_path}")
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(video_path))
    total_frames = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
    fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)

    if total_frames == 0 or fps == 0:
        cap.release()
        return []

    interval = max(1, total_frames // (max_frames * 2))
    selected_frames = []
    prev_frame = None
    frame_idx = 0

    while len(selected_frames) < max_frames:
        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, frame_idx)
        ret, frame = cap.read()

        if not ret:
            break

        if frame.shape[0] > 720:
            scale = 720 / frame.shape[0]
            frame = cv2.resize(frame, (int(frame.shape[1] * scale), 720))

        if prev_frame is None:
            selected_frames.append((frame_idx, frame))
            prev_frame = frame
        else:
            diff = calculate_frame_difference(prev_frame, frame)
            if diff > FRAME_DIFFERENCE_THRESHOLD:
                selected_frames.append((frame_idx, frame))
                prev_frame = frame

        frame_idx += interval
        if frame_idx >= total_frames:
            break

    cap.release()
    print(f"Extracted {len(selected_frames)} frames from {total_frames} total frames")
    return selected_frames

def detect_all_faces_in_frame(image):
    h, w = image.shape[:2]
    all_detections = []
    seen_boxes = []

    def box_iou(b1, b2):
        x1 = max(b1[0], b2[0])
        y1 = max(b1[1], b2[1])
        x2 = min(b1[0]+b1[2], b2[0]+b2[2])
        y2 = min(b1[1]+b1[3], b2[1]+b2[3])
        inter = max(0, x2-x1) * max(0, y2-y1)
        union = b1[2]*b1[3] + b2[2]*b2[3] - inter
        return inter / (union + 1e-6)

    def already_seen(bbox, threshold=0.35):
        for sb in seen_boxes:
            if box_iou(bbox, sb) > threshold:
                return True
        return False

    if FACE_RECOGNITION_AVAILABLE:
        for upsample in [1, 2]:
            try:
                rgb = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
                locs = face_recognition.face_locations(rgb, number_of_times_to_upsample=upsample, model='hog')
                encs = face_recognition.face_encodings(rgb, locs, num_jitters=0)
                for (top, right, bottom, left), enc in zip(locs, encs):
                    pad_y = int((bottom - top) * 0.15)
                    pad_x = int((right - left) * 0.10)
                    top2 = max(0, top - pad_y)
                    left2 = max(0, left - pad_x)
                    bottom2 = min(h, bottom + pad_y)
                    right2 = min(w, right + pad_x)
                    bbox = (left2, top2, right2 - left2, bottom2 - top2)
                    if not already_seen(bbox):
                        face_roi = image[top2:bottom2, left2:right2]
                        quality = (right - left) * (bottom - top) / (w * h)
                        all_detections.append({
                            'bbox': bbox,
                            'encoding': enc.tolist(),
                            'face_image': face_roi,
                            'quality_score': quality,
                            'source': 'face_recognition'
                        })
                        seen_boxes.append(bbox)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"face_recognition upsample={upsample} error: {e}")

    if MEDIAPIPE_AVAILABLE:
        try:
            fd = mp.solutions.face_detection.FaceDetection(model_selection=0, min_detection_confidence=0.4)
            rgb = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
            res = fd.process(rgb)
            if res.detections:
                for det in res.detections:
                    bb = det.location_data.relative_bounding_box
                    x = max(0, int(bb.xmin * w))
                    y = max(0, int(bb.ymin * h))
                    bw = min(w - x, int(bb.width * w))
                    bh = min(h - y, int(bb.height * h))
                    if bw < 15 or bh < 15:
                        continue
                    bbox = (x, y, bw, bh)
                    if not already_seen(bbox):
                        face_roi = image[y:y+bh, x:x+bw]
                        if FACE_RECOGNITION_AVAILABLE and face_roi.size > 0:
                            try:
                                rgb_roi = cv2.cvtColor(face_roi, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
                                encs = face_recognition.face_encodings(rgb_roi, num_jitters=0)
                                enc = encs[0].tolist() if encs else cv2.resize(face_roi, (128,128)).flatten()[:128].tolist()
                            except:
                                enc = cv2.resize(face_roi, (128,128)).flatten()[:128].tolist()
                        else:
                            enc = cv2.resize(face_roi, (128,128)).flatten()[:128].tolist() if face_roi.size > 0 else [0]*128
                        score = float(det.score[0]) if hasattr(det, 'score') and det.score else 0.5
                        all_detections.append({
                            'bbox': bbox,
                            'encoding': enc,
                            'face_image': face_roi,
                            'quality_score': score,
                            'source': 'mediapipe'
                        })
                        seen_boxes.append(bbox)
            fd.close()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"MediaPipe face detection error: {e}")

    if len(all_detections) == 0:
        try:
            cascade_path = cv2.data.haarcascades + 'haarcascade_frontalface_default.xml'
            alt2_path = cv2.data.haarcascades + 'haarcascade_frontalface_alt2.xml'
            gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
            for cpath, sf, mn in [(cascade_path, 1.05, 4), (alt2_path, 1.05, 3)]:
                try:
                    cascade = cv2.CascadeClassifier(cpath)
                    dets = cascade.detectMultiScale(gray, scaleFactor=sf, minNeighbors=mn,
                                                    minSize=(20, 20), flags=cv2.CASCADE_SCALE_IMAGE)
                    if len(dets) > 0:
                        for (x, y, fw, fh) in dets:
                            bbox = (int(x), int(y), int(fw), int(fh))
                            if not already_seen(bbox):
                                face_roi = image[y:y+fh, x:x+fw]
                                if face_roi.size == 0:
                                    continue
                                if FACE_RECOGNITION_AVAILABLE:
                                    try:
                                        rgb_roi = cv2.cvtColor(face_roi, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
                                        encs = face_recognition.face_encodings(rgb_roi, num_jitters=0)
                                        enc = encs[0].tolist() if encs else cv2.resize(face_roi,(128,128)).flatten()[:128].tolist()
                                    except:
                                        enc = cv2.resize(face_roi,(128,128)).flatten()[:128].tolist()
                                else:
                                    enc = cv2.resize(face_roi,(128,128)).flatten()[:128].tolist()
                                all_detections.append({
                                    'bbox': bbox,
                                    'encoding': enc,
                                    'face_image': face_roi,
                                    'quality_score': (fw * fh) / (w * h),
                                    'source': 'haar'
                                })
                                seen_boxes.append(bbox)
                except:
                    pass
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Cascade error: {e}")

    print(f"  Detected {len(all_detections)} faces in frame")
    return all_detections

def analyze_posture_for_person(full_frame, face_bbox):
    h, w = full_frame.shape[:2]
    fx, fy, fw, fh = face_bbox

    body_x1 = max(0, fx - int(fw * 0.8))
    body_y1 = max(0, fy)
    body_x2 = min(w, fx + fw + int(fw * 0.8))
    body_y2 = min(h, fy + fh * 5)

    person_crop = full_frame[body_y1:body_y2, body_x1:body_x2]

    posture_data = {
        'shoulder_symmetry': 50,
        'head_forward_tilt': 50,
        'spine_alignment': 50,
        'body_openness': 50,
        'movement_energy': 50,
        'posture_quality': 'unknown',
        'posture_score': 50,
        'energy_level': 50,
        'landmarks': None
    }

    if not MEDIAPIPE_AVAILABLE or person_crop.size == 0 or person_crop.shape[0] < 40 or person_crop.shape[1] < 20:
        return posture_data

    try:
        pose = mp_pose.Pose(static_image_mode=True, model_complexity=0,
                            min_detection_confidence=0.4, min_tracking_confidence=0.4)
        enhanced_crop = enhance_face_crop(person_crop)
        rgb = cv2.cvtColor(enhanced_crop, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        results = pose.process(rgb)
        pose.close()

        if results.pose_landmarks:
            lm = results.pose_landmarks.landmark
            L = mp_pose.PoseLandmark

            ls = lm[L.LEFT_SHOULDER.value]
            rs = lm[L.RIGHT_SHOULDER.value]
            lh = lm[L.LEFT_HIP.value]
            rh = lm[L.RIGHT_HIP.value]
            nose = lm[L.NOSE.value]
            le = lm[L.LEFT_EAR.value]
            re = lm[L.RIGHT_EAR.value]
            lw = lm[L.LEFT_WRIST.value]
            rw = lm[L.RIGHT_WRIST.value]
            le_elbow = lm[L.LEFT_ELBOW.value]
            re_elbow = lm[L.RIGHT_ELBOW.value]

            shoulder_diff = abs(ls.y - rs.y)
            shoulder_symmetry = max(0, 100 - shoulder_diff * 400)

            mid_shoulder_x = (ls.x + rs.x) / 2
            head_offset = abs(nose.x - mid_shoulder_x)
            head_forward_tilt = max(0, 100 - head_offset * 300)

            mid_hip_x = (lh.x + rh.x) / 2
            spine_offset = abs(mid_shoulder_x - mid_hip_x)
            spine_alignment = max(0, 100 - spine_offset * 350)

            shoulder_width = abs(ls.x - rs.x)
            hip_width = abs(lh.x - rh.x)
            openness_ratio = shoulder_width / (hip_width + 1e-6)
            body_openness = min(100, max(0, openness_ratio * 60))

            wrist_heights = [(1 - lw.y), (1 - rw.y)]
            elbow_heights = [(1 - le_elbow.y), (1 - re_elbow.y)]
            arm_activity = np.mean(wrist_heights + elbow_heights) * 100
            movement_energy = min(100, max(0, arm_activity * 1.4))

            posture_score = (
                shoulder_symmetry * 0.25 +
                head_forward_tilt * 0.20 +
                spine_alignment * 0.25 +
                body_openness * 0.15 +
                movement_energy * 0.15
            )
            posture_score = float(np.clip(posture_score, 0, 100))

            if posture_score >= 78:
                posture_quality = 'excellent'
                energy_level = int(75 + movement_energy * 0.25)
            elif posture_score >= 60:
                posture_quality = 'good'
                energy_level = int(60 + movement_energy * 0.20)
            elif posture_score >= 42:
                posture_quality = 'fair'
                energy_level = int(45 + movement_energy * 0.15)
            else:
                posture_quality = 'poor'
                energy_level = int(25 + movement_energy * 0.15)

            posture_data = {
                'shoulder_symmetry': int(shoulder_symmetry),
                'head_forward_tilt': int(head_forward_tilt),
                'spine_alignment': int(spine_alignment),
                'body_openness': int(body_openness),
                'movement_energy': int(movement_energy),
                'posture_quality': posture_quality,
                'posture_score': round(posture_score, 1),
                'energy_level': min(100, int(energy_level)),
                'landmarks': [(l.x, l.y, l.z) for l in lm]
            }
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Per-person posture error: {e}")

    return posture_data

def analyze_face_texture_traits(face_image):
    traits = {
        'face_brightness': 50,
        'facial_tension': 50,
        'eye_openness': 50,
        'skin_tone_uniformity': 50
    }

    if face_image is None or face_image.size == 0:
        return traits

    try:
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(face_image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        resized = cv2.resize(face_image, (96, 96))
        gray_r = cv2.cvtColor(resized, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

        brightness = float(np.mean(gray))
        traits['face_brightness'] = int(np.clip(brightness / 2.55, 0, 100))

        laplacian = cv2.Laplacian(gray_r, cv2.CV_64F)
        tension = float(np.std(laplacian))
        traits['facial_tension'] = int(np.clip(tension / 3, 0, 100))

        h, w = gray_r.shape
        eye_region = gray_r[int(h*0.2):int(h*0.55), :]
        eye_brightness = float(np.mean(eye_region))
        traits['eye_openness'] = int(np.clip(eye_brightness / 2.55, 0, 100))

        hsv = cv2.cvtColor(resized, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
        sat = hsv[:, :, 1]
        skin_uniformity = 100 - int(np.clip(float(np.std(sat)) / 1.28, 0, 100))
        traits['skin_tone_uniformity'] = skin_uniformity

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Face texture analysis error: {e}")

    return traits

def analyze_emotion_full(face_image):
    emotion_data = {
        'dominant_emotion': 'neutral',
        'emotion_scores': {
            'happy': 0, 'neutral': 60, 'sad': 0,
            'angry': 0, 'fear': 0, 'surprise': 0, 'disgust': 0
        },
        'engagement': 50,
        'stress_level': 'low',
        'emotional_positivity': 50,
        'emotional_stability': 60,
        'social_confidence': 50,
    }

    if face_image is None or face_image.size == 0:
        return emotion_data

    if DEEPFACE_AVAILABLE:
        try:
            enhanced = enhance_face_crop(face_image) if face_image.shape[0] >= 40 else face_image
            result = DeepFace.analyze(enhanced, actions=['emotion'],
                                       enforce_detection=False, silent=True)
            if isinstance(result, list):
                result = result[0]

            em = result['emotion']
            emotion_data['dominant_emotion'] = result['dominant_emotion']
            emotion_data['emotion_scores'] = {k: round(float(v), 2) for k, v in em.items()}

            happy = em.get('happy', 0)
            neutral = em.get('neutral', 0)
            sad = em.get('sad', 0)
            angry = em.get('angry', 0)
            fear = em.get('fear', 0)
            surprise = em.get('surprise', 0)
            disgust = em.get('disgust', 0)

            positive = happy + neutral * 0.5 + surprise * 0.3
            negative = sad * 1.2 + angry * 1.4 + fear * 1.3 + disgust * 1.1

            emotional_positivity = np.clip(50 + (positive - negative) * 0.45, 0, 100)
            engagement = np.clip((happy * 1.3 + surprise * 0.9 + neutral * 0.6) * 0.6, 0, 100)
            stress_raw = (angry * 1.5 + fear * 1.4 + sad * 0.9 + disgust * 0.8) / 100
            stability = np.clip(100 - stress_raw * 120, 0, 100)
            social_conf = np.clip((happy * 1.1 + neutral * 0.7 - sad * 0.8 - fear * 0.9) * 0.5 + 40, 0, 100)

            emotion_data['engagement'] = int(engagement)
            emotion_data['stress_level'] = 'high' if stress_raw > 0.35 else 'moderate' if stress_raw > 0.15 else 'low'
            emotion_data['emotional_positivity'] = int(emotional_positivity)
            emotion_data['emotional_stability'] = int(stability)
            emotion_data['social_confidence'] = int(social_conf)

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"DeepFace emotion error: {e}")

    else:
        try:
            texture = analyze_face_texture_traits(face_image)
            brightness_score = texture['face_brightness']
            tension_score = texture['facial_tension']

            positivity = np.clip(brightness_score * 0.7 + (100 - tension_score) * 0.3, 0, 100)
            stability = np.clip((100 - tension_score) * 0.8 + brightness_score * 0.2, 0, 100)
            engagement_est = np.clip(brightness_score * 0.6 + texture['eye_openness'] * 0.4, 0, 100)

            emotion_data['emotional_positivity'] = int(positivity)
            emotion_data['emotional_stability'] = int(stability)
            emotion_data['engagement'] = int(engagement_est)
            emotion_data['social_confidence'] = int(positivity * 0.8)
            emotion_data['stress_level'] = 'high' if tension_score > 65 else 'moderate' if tension_score > 40 else 'low'

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Fallback emotion error: {e}")

    return emotion_data

def compute_10_trait_wellbeing(emotion_data, posture_data, texture_traits):
    traits = {}

    traits['emotional_positivity'] = int(np.clip(emotion_data.get('emotional_positivity', 50), 0, 100))
    traits['stress_resilience'] = int(np.clip(emotion_data.get('emotional_stability', 60), 0, 100))
    traits['social_engagement'] = int(np.clip(emotion_data.get('engagement', 50), 0, 100))
    traits['social_confidence'] = int(np.clip(emotion_data.get('social_confidence', 50), 0, 100))
    traits['physical_energy'] = int(np.clip(posture_data.get('energy_level', 50), 0, 100))
    traits['posture_health'] = int(np.clip(posture_data.get('posture_score', 50), 0, 100))
    traits['body_openness'] = int(np.clip(posture_data.get('body_openness', 50), 0, 100))
    traits['focus_alertness'] = int(np.clip(
        (texture_traits.get('eye_openness', 50) * 0.6 + posture_data.get('head_forward_tilt', 50) * 0.4),
        0, 100
    ))
    traits['facial_relaxation'] = int(np.clip(100 - texture_traits.get('facial_tension', 50), 0, 100))
    traits['vitality_glow'] = int(np.clip(
        (texture_traits.get('face_brightness', 50) * 0.5 + texture_traits.get('skin_tone_uniformity', 50) * 0.5),
        0, 100
    ))

    weights = {
        'emotional_positivity': 0.18,
        'stress_resilience': 0.14,
        'social_engagement': 0.10,
        'social_confidence': 0.08,
        'physical_energy': 0.14,
        'posture_health': 0.12,
        'body_openness': 0.06,
        'focus_alertness': 0.10,
        'facial_relaxation': 0.04,
        'vitality_glow': 0.04,
    }

    overall = sum(traits[k] * weights[k] for k in weights)
    overall = int(np.clip(overall, 0, 100))

    stress = emotion_data.get('stress_level', 'low')
    stress_penalty = {'high': 8, 'moderate': 3, 'low': 0}.get(stress, 0)
    overall = max(0, overall - stress_penalty)

    return traits, overall

def calculate_person_similarity(encoding1, encoding2):
    if encoding1 is None or encoding2 is None:
        return 0.0
    try:
        enc1 = np.array(encoding1, dtype=np.float64)
        enc2 = np.array(encoding2, dtype=np.float64)

        if len(enc1) == 128 and len(enc2) == 128:
            distance = np.linalg.norm(enc1 - enc2)
            similarity = max(0.0, 1.0 - distance / 0.8)
            return float(similarity)

        if len(enc1) != len(enc2):
            min_len = min(len(enc1), len(enc2))
            enc1 = enc1[:min_len]
            enc2 = enc2[:min_len]

        norm1 = np.linalg.norm(enc1)
        norm2 = np.linalg.norm(enc2)
        if norm1 == 0 or norm2 == 0:
            return 0.0
        similarity = np.dot(enc1, enc2) / (norm1 * norm2 + 1e-6)
        return float(np.clip(similarity, 0.0, 1.0))
    except Exception:
        return 0.0

def match_or_create_person(face_data, frame_image, timestamp, date_str, school_name='Unknown School'):
    encoding = face_data['encoding']

    best_match = None
    best_similarity = 0

    for person_id, person_info in person_database.items():
        if person_info.get('school') != school_name:
            continue
        if 'encoding' in person_info:
            similarity = calculate_person_similarity(encoding, person_info['encoding'])
            if similarity > best_similarity and similarity > SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD:
                best_similarity = similarity
                best_match = person_id

    if best_match:
        person_database[best_match]['last_seen'] = timestamp
        person_database[best_match]['appearance_count'] += 1

        if date_str not in person_database[best_match]['dates_seen']:
            person_database[best_match]['dates_seen'].append(date_str)

        if face_data['quality_score'] > person_database[best_match].get('best_quality', 0):
            person_database[best_match]['best_quality'] = face_data['quality_score']
            profile_image = create_passport_image(face_data['face_image'])
            person_database[best_match]['profile_image'] = profile_image

        return best_match
    else:
        school_persons = [p for p in person_database.values() if p.get('school') == school_name]
        person_id = f"{school_name.replace(' ','_').upper()}_P{len(school_persons) + 1:04d}"
        profile_image = create_passport_image(face_data['face_image'])

        names_list = [
            "Alex Chen", "Jordan Blake", "Morgan Silva", "Taylor Reed",
            "Casey Wright", "Riley Storm", "Drew Patel", "Quinn Davis",
            "Avery Stone", "Parker Lane", "Skyler Fox", "Cameron Wells",
            "Bailey Ross", "Rowan Hart", "Finley Nash", "Sage Kumar",
            "Noel Sharma", "River Das", "Emery Nair", "Phoenix Iyer"
        ]
        name_idx = len(person_database) % len(names_list)
        auto_name = names_list[name_idx]

        person_database[person_id] = {
            'person_id': person_id,
            'name': auto_name,
            'school': school_name,
            'encoding': encoding,
            'first_seen': timestamp,
            'last_seen': timestamp,
            'dates_seen': [date_str],
            'appearance_count': 1,
            'profile_image': profile_image,
            'best_quality': face_data['quality_score'],
            'is_name_editable': True
        }

        print(f"New person registered: {auto_name} ({person_id}) at {school_name}")
        return person_id

def score_frame_quality(frame, face_bbox):
    """
    Score quality of the full frame around this face detection.
    Uses the face region for sharpness/brightness but rewards larger body visible area.
    """
    if frame is None or frame.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    try:
        fx, fy, fw, fh = face_bbox
        fh_img, fw_img = frame.shape[:2]

        # Use face region for sharpness (most informative area)
        face_roi = frame[fy:fy+fh, fx:fx+fw]
        if face_roi.size == 0:
            return 0.0

        gray_face = cv2.cvtColor(face_roi, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

        # 1. Sharpness of face region
        lap_var = float(cv2.Laplacian(gray_face, cv2.CV_64F).var())
        sharpness_score = min(100.0, lap_var / 4.0)

        # 2. Face size relative to frame — bigger face = person closer = more detail
        face_area_ratio = (fw * fh) / (fw_img * fh_img + 1e-6)
        size_score = min(100.0, face_area_ratio * 2000.0)

        # 3. Brightness balance of face
        mean_brightness = float(np.mean(gray_face))
        brightness_score = max(0.0, 100.0 - abs(mean_brightness - 120) * 1.3)

        # 4. Contrast
        contrast_score = min(100.0, float(np.std(gray_face)) * 2.2)

        # 5. Symmetry of face region
        h_f, w_f = gray_face.shape
        if w_f > 10:
            left_h  = gray_face[:, :w_f//2]
            right_h = cv2.flip(gray_face[:, w_f - w_f//2:], 1)
            min_ww  = min(left_h.shape[1], right_h.shape[1])
            sym_diff = np.mean(np.abs(left_h[:, :min_ww].astype(float) - right_h[:, :min_ww].astype(float)))
            symmetry_score = max(0.0, 100.0 - sym_diff * 2.5)
        else:
            symmetry_score = 40.0

        # 6. Body visibility — how much frame is available below the face (torso)
        body_available = min(1.0, (fh_img - fy - fh) / max(fh * 2, 1))
        body_score = min(100.0, body_available * 120.0)

        total = (
            sharpness_score  * 0.32 +
            size_score       * 0.22 +
            symmetry_score   * 0.18 +
            brightness_score * 0.12 +
            contrast_score   * 0.08 +
            body_score       * 0.08
        )
        return float(total)
    except:
        return 0.0

def crop_person_from_frame(frame, face_bbox):
    """
    Crop the FULL PERSON (head + shoulders + torso) from the original frame.
    Does NOT use the tiny face crop — uses the full HD frame pixel data directly.
    Returns the person crop ready to be saved as profile image.
    """
    if frame is None or frame.size == 0:
        return None
    try:
        fh_img, fw_img = frame.shape[:2]
        fx, fy, fw, fh = face_bbox

        # Estimate full body bounding box from face position
        # Head padding: extend upward ~30% of face height for hair/top of head
        # Body: extend downward ~3× face height to show shoulders + upper torso
        # Width: extend sideways ~80% of face width on each side for shoulders

        pad_top    = int(fh * 0.35)
        pad_sides  = int(fw * 0.85)
        body_down  = int(fh * 3.2)   # torso below chin

        x1 = max(0, fx - pad_sides)
        y1 = max(0, fy - pad_top)
        x2 = min(fw_img, fx + fw + pad_sides)
        y2 = min(fh_img, fy + fh + body_down)

        # Ensure minimum crop size
        if (x2 - x1) < 20 or (y2 - y1) < 30:
            return None

        person_crop = frame[y1:y2, x1:x2].copy()
        return person_crop
    except:
        return None

def enhance_person_crop(person_crop):
    """
    Enhance a full-person CCTV crop for clarity without distorting it.
    Uses CLAHE for contrast, gentle unsharp mask for sharpness, NO heavy denoising.
    """
    if person_crop is None or person_crop.size == 0:
        return person_crop
    try:
        # CLAHE contrast enhancement
        lab = cv2.cvtColor(person_crop, cv2.COLOR_BGR2LAB)
        l, a, b = cv2.split(lab)
        l = _clahe.apply(l)
        enhanced = cv2.cvtColor(cv2.merge([l, a, b]), cv2.COLOR_LAB2BGR)

        # Unsharp mask — recover edge detail lost in CCTV compression
        blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(enhanced, (0, 0), sigmaX=1.2)
        enhanced = cv2.addWeighted(enhanced, 1.45, blur, -0.45, 0)

        return enhanced
    except:
        return person_crop

def create_profile_image(person_crop):
    """
    Take a full-person crop (head+body), enhance it, upscale if needed,
    and save as a clean 300×400 portrait profile image.
    """
    blank_h, blank_w = 400, 300
    blank = np.ones((blank_h, blank_w, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 228
    blank_b64 = base64.b64encode(cv2.imencode('.jpg', blank)[1]).decode('utf-8')

    if person_crop is None or person_crop.size == 0:
        return blank_b64
    try:
        h, w = person_crop.shape[:2]
        if h < 20 or w < 10:
            return blank_b64

        # Enhance the raw crop
        enhanced = enhance_person_crop(person_crop)

        # Target portrait canvas: 300 wide × 400 tall
        target_w, target_h = 300, 400

        # Scale to fit within target while keeping aspect ratio
        scale = min(target_w / w, target_h / h)

        # If very small (CCTV low-res), do 2-step upscale for quality
        if scale > 1.5:
            # Step 1: 2× Lanczos
            step1 = cv2.resize(enhanced, (max(1, w*2), max(1, h*2)),
                               interpolation=cv2.INTER_LANCZOS4)
            sh, sw = step1.shape[:2]
            scale2 = min(target_w / sw, target_h / sh)
            new_w = max(1, int(sw * scale2))
            new_h = max(1, int(sh * scale2))
            resized = cv2.resize(step1, (new_w, new_h),
                                 interpolation=cv2.INTER_LANCZOS4)
        else:
            new_w = max(1, int(w * scale))
            new_h = max(1, int(h * scale))
            resized = cv2.resize(enhanced, (new_w, new_h),
                                 interpolation=cv2.INTER_LANCZOS4)

        # Place on light-grey canvas, centred
        canvas = np.ones((target_h, target_w, 3), dtype=np.uint8) * 228
        y_off = (target_h - new_h) // 2
        x_off = (target_w - new_w) // 2
        canvas[y_off:y_off+new_h, x_off:x_off+new_w] = resized

        _, buf = cv2.imencode('.jpg', canvas, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 95])
        return base64.b64encode(buf).decode('utf-8')
    except:
        return blank_b64

def match_or_create_person(face_data, frame_image, timestamp, date_str, school_name='Unknown School'):
    encoding  = face_data['encoding']
    face_bbox = face_data['bbox']                          # (x, y, w, h) in full frame
    quality   = score_frame_quality(frame_image, face_bbox)

    best_match      = None
    best_similarity = 0

    for person_id, person_info in person_database.items():
        if person_info.get('school') != school_name:
            continue
        stored_encs = person_info.get('all_encodings', [])
        if not stored_encs and 'encoding' in person_info:
            stored_encs = [person_info['encoding']]
        for stored_enc in stored_encs:
            if stored_enc is None:
                continue
            sim = calculate_person_similarity(encoding, stored_enc)
            if sim > best_similarity and sim > SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD:
                best_similarity = sim
                best_match = person_id

    if best_match:
        pid = best_match
        person_database[pid]['last_seen'] = timestamp
        person_database[pid]['appearance_count'] += 1
        if date_str not in person_database[pid]['dates_seen']:
            person_database[pid]['dates_seen'].append(date_str)
        all_encs = person_database[pid].get('all_encodings', [])
        if len(all_encs) < 8:
            all_encs.append(encoding)
            person_database[pid]['all_encodings'] = all_encs
        # Replace profile photo only if this frame is sharper / clearer
        if quality > person_database[pid].get('best_quality', 0):
            person_crop = crop_person_from_frame(frame_image, face_bbox)
            if person_crop is not None:
                person_database[pid]['best_quality']  = quality
                person_database[pid]['profile_image'] = create_profile_image(person_crop)
                print(f"  Updated body photo for {pid} (quality={quality:.1f})")
        return pid
    else:
        school_persons = [p for p in person_database.values() if p.get('school') == school_name]
        person_id = f"{school_name.replace(' ','_').upper()}_P{len(school_persons) + 1:04d}"
        names_list = [
            "Alex Chen", "Jordan Blake", "Morgan Silva", "Taylor Reed",
            "Casey Wright", "Riley Storm", "Drew Patel", "Quinn Davis",
            "Avery Stone", "Parker Lane", "Skyler Fox", "Cameron Wells",
            "Bailey Ross", "Rowan Hart", "Finley Nash", "Sage Kumar",
            "Noel Sharma", "River Das", "Emery Nair", "Phoenix Iyer",
            "Arjun Mehta", "Priya Rajan", "Kiran Bose", "Ananya Pillai",
            "Rohan Verma", "Diya Kapoor", "Vikram Singh", "Tara Rao",
            "Aditya Joshi", "Meera Nair"
        ]
        auto_name = names_list[len(person_database) % len(names_list)]

        person_crop  = crop_person_from_frame(frame_image, face_bbox)
        profile_img  = create_profile_image(person_crop) if person_crop is not None else create_profile_image(None)

        person_database[person_id] = {
            'person_id':        person_id,
            'name':             auto_name,
            'school':           school_name,
            'encoding':         encoding,
            'all_encodings':    [encoding],
            'first_seen':       timestamp,
            'last_seen':        timestamp,
            'dates_seen':       [date_str],
            'appearance_count': 1,
            'profile_image':    profile_img,
            'best_quality':     quality,
            'is_name_editable': True
        }
        print(f"New person registered: {auto_name} ({person_id}) at {school_name}")
        return person_id

_posture_call_counter = 0

def analyze_frame(frame, frame_idx, timestamp, date_str, school_name='Unknown School'):
    global _posture_call_counter
    frame_analysis = {
        'frame_idx': frame_idx,
        'timestamp': timestamp,
        'persons': []
    }

    faces = detect_all_faces_in_frame(frame)

    if len(faces) == 0:
        print(f"  Frame {frame_idx}: no faces detected")
        return frame_analysis

    print(f"  Frame {frame_idx}: processing {len(faces)} detected faces")

    for face_data in faces:
        person_id = match_or_create_person(face_data, frame, timestamp, date_str, school_name)
        face_img = face_data['face_image']
        bbox = face_data['bbox']

        emotion = analyze_emotion_full(face_img)
        texture = analyze_face_texture_traits(face_img)

        # Run posture every 3rd call to save time — pose is the slowest step
        _posture_call_counter += 1
        if _posture_call_counter % 3 == 1:
            posture = analyze_posture_for_person(frame, bbox)
            _last_posture = posture
        else:
            posture = getattr(analyze_frame, '_last_posture', None) or analyze_posture_for_person(frame, bbox)

        analyze_frame._last_posture = posture

        traits, overall_wellbeing = compute_10_trait_wellbeing(emotion, posture, texture)

        frame_analysis['persons'].append({
            'person_id': person_id,
            'has_face': True,
            'emotion': emotion,
            'posture': posture,
            'texture': texture,
            'traits': traits,
            'overall_wellbeing': overall_wellbeing
        })

    return frame_analysis

def analyze_video_file(video_path, date_str, school_name='Unknown School'):
    print(f"Analyzing video: {video_path.name}")

    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(video_path))
    fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS) or 25.0
    cap.release()

    frames = extract_intelligent_frames(video_path)

    video_analysis = {
        'video_name': video_path.name,
        'date': date_str,
        'school': school_name,
        'total_frames_analyzed': len(frames),
        'fps': fps,
        'frames': []
    }

    for frame_idx, frame in frames:
        frame_time_sec = round(frame_idx / fps, 2)
        timestamp = datetime.now().isoformat()
        frame_result = analyze_frame(frame, frame_idx, timestamp, date_str, school_name)
        frame_result['frame_time_sec'] = frame_time_sec
        video_analysis['frames'].append(frame_result)

    return video_analysis

def analyze_date_folder(date_folder, school_name='Unknown School'):
    date_str = date_folder.name
    print(f"  Date: {date_str} | School: {school_name}")

    video_files = (list(date_folder.glob("*.mp4")) + list(date_folder.glob("*.avi")) +
                   list(date_folder.glob("*.mov")) + list(date_folder.glob("*.mkv")) +
                   list(date_folder.glob("*.MP4")) + list(date_folder.glob("*.MOV")) +
                   list(date_folder.glob("*.AVI")) + list(date_folder.glob("*.MKV")) +
                   list(date_folder.glob("*.webm")) + list(date_folder.glob("*.flv")))

    video_files = list(set(video_files))

    if not video_files:
        print(f"  No video files found in {date_str}")
        return None

    date_analysis = {
        'date': date_str,
        'school': school_name,
        'videos': [],
        'summary': {}
    }

    for video_file in video_files:
        video_result = analyze_video_file(video_file, date_str, school_name)
        date_analysis['videos'].append(video_result)

    all_persons = set()
    total_wellbeing = []

    for video in date_analysis['videos']:
        for frame in video['frames']:
            for person in frame['persons']:
                all_persons.add(person['person_id'])
                total_wellbeing.append(person['overall_wellbeing'])

    date_analysis['summary'] = {
        'unique_persons': len(all_persons),
        'average_wellbeing': int(np.mean(total_wellbeing)) if total_wellbeing else 0,
        'total_detections': len(total_wellbeing)
    }

    safe_school = school_name.replace(' ', '_').replace('/', '-')
    output_file = ANALYSIS_DIR / f"analysis_{safe_school}_{date_str}.json"
    with open(output_file, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(date_analysis, f, indent=2)

    print(f"  Saved: {output_file.name}")
    return date_analysis

def analyze_all_dates():
    print("Starting Sentio Mind analysis pipeline")

    school_folders = sorted([d for d in INPUT_VIDEOS_DIR.iterdir() if d.is_dir()])

    if not school_folders:
        print("No school folders found in input_videos/")
        return {}

    all_results = {}

    for school_folder in school_folders:
        school_name = school_folder.name
        print(f"\nSchool: {school_name}")

        date_folders = sorted([d for d in school_folder.iterdir() if d.is_dir()])

        if not date_folders:
            print(f"  No date folders found inside {school_name}")
            continue

        date_folders = date_folders[-5:]

        for date_folder in date_folders:
            result = analyze_date_folder(date_folder, school_name)
            if result:
                key = f"{school_name}|{result['date']}"
                all_results[key] = result

    profiles_file = PROFILES_DIR / "person_database.json"
    with open(profiles_file, 'w') as f:
        serializable_db = {}
        for pid, pdata in person_database.items():
            serializable_db[pid] = {
                'person_id': pdata['person_id'],
                'name': pdata['name'],
                'school': pdata.get('school', 'Unknown School'),
                'first_seen': pdata['first_seen'],
                'last_seen': pdata['last_seen'],
                'dates_seen': pdata['dates_seen'],
                'appearance_count': pdata['appearance_count'],
                'profile_image': pdata['profile_image'],
                'best_sharpness': pdata.get('best_sharpness', 0),
                'is_name_editable': pdata.get('is_name_editable', True)
            }
        json.dump(serializable_db, f, indent=2)

    print(f"\nTotal unique persons tracked: {len(person_database)}")
    return all_results

def generate_multi_day_report():
    date_results = {}

    for analysis_file in ANALYSIS_DIR.glob("analysis_*.json"):
        with open(analysis_file, 'r') as f:
            data = json.load(f)
            school = data.get('school', 'Unknown School')
            date = data.get('date', '')
            key = f"{school}|{date}"
            date_results[key] = data

    TRAIT_KEYS = [
        'emotional_positivity', 'stress_resilience', 'social_engagement',
        'social_confidence', 'physical_energy', 'posture_health',
        'body_openness', 'focus_alertness', 'facial_relaxation', 'vitality_glow'
    ]

    person_timeline = defaultdict(lambda: {
        'dates': [],
        'wellbeing_scores': [],
        'engagement_scores': [],
        'detections': 0,
        'trait_sums': {k: [] for k in TRAIT_KEYS},
        'temporal_series': []   # [{t, date, video, wellbeing, traits}, ...]
    })

    all_schools = set()
    all_dates = set()

    for key, date_data in date_results.items():
        school = date_data.get('school', 'Unknown School')
        date = date_data.get('date', '')
        all_schools.add(school)
        all_dates.add(date)
        for video in date_data['videos']:
            video_name = video.get('video_name', '')
            for frame in video['frames']:
                frame_t = frame.get('frame_time_sec', frame.get('frame_idx', 0))
                for person in frame['persons']:
                    pid = person['person_id']
                    if pid.startswith('NOBODYFACE'):
                        continue
                    wb = person['overall_wellbeing']
                    tr = person.get('traits', {})
                    person_timeline[pid]['dates'].append(date)
                    person_timeline[pid]['wellbeing_scores'].append(wb)
                    person_timeline[pid]['engagement_scores'].append(
                        person.get('emotion', {}).get('engagement', 50)
                    )
                    person_timeline[pid]['detections'] += 1
                    for tk in TRAIT_KEYS:
                        val = tr.get(tk)
                        if val is not None:
                            person_timeline[pid]['trait_sums'][tk].append(val)
                    # Build temporal point — label = "date  Xs"
                    person_timeline[pid]['temporal_series'].append({
                        't': float(frame_t),
                        'date': date,
                        'video': video_name,
                        'label': f"{date} {frame_t}s",
                        'wellbeing': wb,
                        'traits': {tk: tr.get(tk, 50) for tk in TRAIT_KEYS}
                    })

    # Sort temporal series by date then time
    for pid in person_timeline:
        person_timeline[pid]['temporal_series'].sort(
            key=lambda x: (x['date'], x['t'])
        )

    multi_day_report = {
        'date_summaries': date_results,
        'person_profiles': {},
        'overall_stats': {
            'total_dates_analyzed': len(all_dates),
            'total_unique_persons': len(person_timeline),
            'total_schools': len(all_schools),
            'schools': sorted(list(all_schools)),
            'date_range': sorted(list(all_dates))
        }
    }

    for pid, timeline in person_timeline.items():
        person_info = person_database.get(pid, {})
        days_present = len(set(timeline['dates']))
        avg_wellbeing = int(np.mean(timeline['wellbeing_scores'])) if timeline['wellbeing_scores'] else 0
        avg_engagement = int(np.mean(timeline['engagement_scores'])) if timeline['engagement_scores'] else 0

        avg_traits = {}
        for tk in TRAIT_KEYS:
            vals = timeline['trait_sums'][tk]
            avg_traits[tk] = int(np.mean(vals)) if vals else 50

        multi_day_report['person_profiles'][pid] = {
            'person_id': pid,
            'name': person_info.get('name', f'Person {pid}'),
            'school': person_info.get('school', 'Unknown School'),
            'profile_image': person_info.get('profile_image', ''),
            'days_present': days_present,
            'total_detections': timeline['detections'],
            'average_wellbeing': avg_wellbeing,
            'average_engagement': avg_engagement,
            'avg_traits': avg_traits,
            'dates_seen': sorted(list(set(timeline['dates']))),
            'wellbeing_trend': timeline['wellbeing_scores'],
            'temporal_series': timeline['temporal_series'],
            'is_name_editable': person_info.get('is_name_editable', True)
        }

    report_file = ANALYSIS_DIR / "multi_day_report.json"
    with open(report_file, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(multi_day_report, f, indent=2)

    print(f"Multi-day report generated: {report_file}")
    return multi_day_report

<script>
let currentData = {};
let editingPersonId = null;

function goTo(page, el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.page-section').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('page-' + page).classList.add('active');
  if (el) el.classList.add('active');
}

function goToAndAnalyze() {
  const btn = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link')[1];
  goTo('dashboard', btn);
  setTimeout(runAnalysis, 200);
}

async function runAnalysis() {
  document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('runBtn').disabled = true;
  try {
    const resp = await fetch('/run_analysis', { method: 'POST' });
    const data = await resp.json();
    if (data.success) {
      currentData = data.report;
      renderAll(data.report);
      document.getElementById('dashDateSub').textContent = 'Last updated: ' + new Date().toLocaleString();
    } else {
      alert('Error: ' + data.message);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    alert('Error running analysis: ' + e);
  } finally {
    document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').classList.remove('active');
    document.getElementById('runBtn').disabled = false;
  }
}

function renderAll(report) {
  renderKPIs(report);
  renderTopPersons(report);
  renderProfiles(report);
  renderTemporalSetup(report);
  renderDaily(report);
  renderTimeline(report);
}

const TRAIT_COLORS = {
  emotional_positivity: '#f59e0b',
  stress_resilience:    '#10b981',
  social_engagement:    '#3b82f6',
  social_confidence:    '#8b5cf6',
  physical_energy:      '#ef4444',
  posture_health:       '#06b6d4',
  body_openness:        '#84cc16',
  focus_alertness:      '#f97316',
  facial_relaxation:    '#ec4899',
  vitality_glow:        '#a78bfa',
  wellbeing:            '#2563eb'
};
const TRAIT_LABELS_MAP = {
  emotional_positivity: '😊 Emotional Positivity',
  stress_resilience:    '🛡️ Stress Resilience',
  social_engagement:    '🤝 Social Engagement',
  social_confidence:    '💬 Social Confidence',
  physical_energy:      '⚡ Physical Energy',
  posture_health:       '🧍 Posture Health',
  body_openness:        '🙌 Body Openness',
  focus_alertness:      '👁️ Focus & Alertness',
  facial_relaxation:    '😌 Facial Relaxation',
  vitality_glow:        '✨ Vitality & Glow',
  wellbeing:            '📊 Overall Wellbeing'
};
const ALL_TRAITS = Object.keys(TRAIT_COLORS).filter(k => k !== 'wellbeing');

let temporalChartInstances = {};
let dashMiniCharts = {};

function destroyCharts(store) {
  Object.values(store).forEach(c => { try { c.destroy(); } catch(e){} });
  Object.keys(store).forEach(k => delete store[k]);
}

/* ─── TEMPORAL PAGE ─────────────────────────────────────── */

function renderTemporalSetup(report) {
  const sel = document.getElementById('temporalPersonSelect');
  const persons = Object.values(report.person_profiles)
    .sort((a,b) => b.total_detections - a.total_detections);
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">— select a person —</option>';
  persons.forEach(p => {
    const opt = document.createElement('option');
    opt.value = p.person_id;
    opt.textContent = p.name + ' (' + (p.school||'') + ') · ' + p.total_detections + ' detections';
    sel.appendChild(opt);
  });
}

function renderTemporalCharts() {
  if (!currentData) return;
  const pid  = document.getElementById('temporalPersonSelect').value;
  const mode = document.getElementById('temporalTraitSelect').value;
  const area = document.getElementById('temporalChartsArea');

  if (!pid) {
    area.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">👤</div><div class="empty-title">Select a person above</div></div>';
    return;
  }

  const p = currentData.person_profiles[pid];
  if (!p) return;

  // Person header card
  const card = document.getElementById('temporalPersonCard');
  card.style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('temporalAvatar').src = 'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + p.profile_image;
  document.getElementById('temporalName').textContent = p.name;
  document.getElementById('temporalMeta').textContent =
    (p.school||'') + ' · ' + p.total_detections + ' detections · ' + p.days_present + ' days';
  const wbColor = p.average_wellbeing >= 70 ? '#10b981' : p.average_wellbeing >= 50 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
  document.getElementById('temporalWbBadge').innerHTML =
    '<span style="font-family:Syne,sans-serif;font-size:1.4rem;font-weight:800;color:'+wbColor+'">'+p.average_wellbeing+'%</span>' +
    '<span style="font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-left:6px">avg wellbeing</span>';

  const series = p.temporal_series || [];
  if (!series.length) {
    area.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">📉</div><div class="empty-title">No temporal data yet for this person</div></div>';
    return;
  }

  destroyCharts(temporalChartInstances);
  area.innerHTML = '';

  // Group series by date
  const byDate = {};
  series.forEach(pt => {
    if (!byDate[pt.date]) byDate[pt.date] = [];
    byDate[pt.date].push(pt);
  });
  const dates = Object.keys(byDate).sort();

  // Determine which traits to show
  const traitsToShow = mode === 'all' ? ['wellbeing', ...ALL_TRAITS] :
                       mode === 'wellbeing' ? ['wellbeing'] : [mode];

  // Build one section per day
  dates.forEach(date => {
    const pts = byDate[date].sort((a,b) => a.t - b.t);
    const xLabels = pts.map(pt => pt.t.toFixed(1) + 's');

    // Day section wrapper
    const dayDiv = document.createElement('div');
    dayDiv.style.cssText = 'margin-bottom:28px';
    dayDiv.innerHTML = `<div style="font-family:Syne,sans-serif;font-weight:800;font-size:1rem;color:var(--text-primary);
      padding:10px 0 8px;border-bottom:2px solid var(--border);margin-bottom:14px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
      <span style="background:var(--accent);color:white;border-radius:6px;padding:2px 10px;font-size:0.8rem">${date}</span>
      <span style="font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:400;color:var(--text-muted)">${pts.length} data points · ${pts[0].video||''}</span>
    </div>`;
    area.appendChild(dayDiv);

    if (mode === 'all') {
      // Wellbeing chart first
      buildChart(dayDiv, 'wb_'+date+'_'+pid, 'Overall Wellbeing — ' + date, xLabels,
        [makeDS('wellbeing', pts.map(pt => pt.wellbeing), true)], '% Wellbeing');
      // Emotional group
      buildChart(dayDiv, 'emo_'+date+'_'+pid, 'Emotional & Social Traits — ' + date, xLabels,
        ['emotional_positivity','stress_resilience','social_engagement','social_confidence','focus_alertness']
          .map(tk => makeDS(tk, pts.map(pt => pt.traits[tk]??null))));
      // Physical group
      buildChart(dayDiv, 'phy_'+date+'_'+pid, 'Physical & Appearance Traits — ' + date, xLabels,
        ['physical_energy','posture_health','body_openness','facial_relaxation','vitality_glow']
          .map(tk => makeDS(tk, pts.map(pt => pt.traits[tk]??null))));
    } else {
      const datasets = traitsToShow.map(tk =>
        tk === 'wellbeing'
          ? makeDS('wellbeing', pts.map(pt => pt.wellbeing), true)
          : makeDS(tk, pts.map(pt => pt.traits[tk]??null))
      );
      buildChart(dayDiv, mode+'_'+date+'_'+pid,
        (TRAIT_LABELS_MAP[mode]||mode) + ' — ' + date, xLabels, datasets);
    }
  });
}

function makeDS(key, data, fill=false) {
  const color = TRAIT_COLORS[key] || '#64748b';
  return {
    label: TRAIT_LABELS_MAP[key] || key,
    data,
    borderColor: color,
    backgroundColor: fill ? color+'22' : color+'10',
    borderWidth: fill ? 3 : 2,
    pointRadius: 5,
    pointHoverRadius: 7,
    tension: 0.4,
    fill
  };
}

function buildChart(parentEl, id, title, labels, datasets, yTitle) {
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.style.cssText = 'background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:14px;padding:18px 20px;margin-bottom:14px';
  wrap.innerHTML = `<div style="font-size:0.88rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--text-primary);margin-bottom:12px">${title}</div>
    <div style="position:relative;height:200px"><canvas id="c_${id}"></canvas></div>`;
  parentEl.appendChild(wrap);
  const ctx = document.getElementById('c_'+id);
  if (!ctx) return;
  temporalChartInstances['c_'+id] = new Chart(ctx.getContext('2d'), {
    type: 'line',
    data: { labels, datasets },
    options: {
      responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
      interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
      plugins: {
        legend: { position: 'top', labels: { font:{family:'DM Sans',size:11}, boxWidth:12, padding:8 } },
        tooltip: { callbacks: { label: c => ' ' + (c.dataset.label||'') + ': ' + (c.parsed.y??'—') + '%' } }
      },
      scales: {
        x: {
          title: { display: true, text: 'Time (seconds into video)', font:{size:10} },
          ticks: { font:{family:'DM Sans',size:10}, maxRotation:30 },
          grid: { color:'#f0eeea' }
        },
        y: {
          min: 0, max: 100,
          title: { display: true, text: yTitle||'Score (%)', font:{size:10} },
          ticks: { font:{size:10} },
          grid: { color:'#f0eeea' }
        }
      }
    }
  });
}

/* ─── DASHBOARD MINI CHARTS for top students ─────────────── */

function buildMiniChart(canvasId, series, traitKey) {
  const ctx = document.getElementById(canvasId);
  if (!ctx) return;
  if (dashMiniCharts[canvasId]) { try { dashMiniCharts[canvasId].destroy(); } catch(e){} }

  // Group by date, take avg per date for mini sparkline
  const byDate = {};
  series.forEach(pt => {
    if (!byDate[pt.date]) byDate[pt.date] = [];
    const val = traitKey === 'wellbeing' ? pt.wellbeing : (pt.traits[traitKey]??null);
    if (val !== null) byDate[pt.date].push(val);
  });
  const dates = Object.keys(byDate).sort();
  const vals  = dates.map(d => Math.round(byDate[d].reduce((a,v)=>a+v,0)/byDate[d].length));
  const color = TRAIT_COLORS[traitKey] || '#2563eb';

  dashMiniCharts[canvasId] = new Chart(ctx.getContext('2d'), {
    type: 'line',
    data: {
      labels: dates.length > 1 ? dates : (series.map((_,i)=>(i+1)+'s')),
      datasets: [{
        data: dates.length > 1 ? vals : series.map(pt => traitKey==='wellbeing'?pt.wellbeing:(pt.traits[traitKey]??50)),
        borderColor: color, backgroundColor: color+'22',
        borderWidth: 2, pointRadius: 3, tension: 0.4, fill: true
      }]
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
      plugins: { legend:{display:false}, tooltip:{callbacks:{label:c=>c.parsed.y+'%'}} },
      scales: {
        x: { display:false },
        y: { display:false, min:0, max:100 }
      }
    }
  });
}

function renderKPIs(report) {
  const stats = report.overall_stats;
  const persons = Object.values(report.person_profiles);
  const avgW = persons.length ? Math.round(persons.reduce((a,p) => a + p.average_wellbeing, 0) / persons.length) : 0;
  const avgE = persons.length ? Math.round(persons.reduce((a,p) => a + p.average_engagement, 0) / persons.length) : 0;
  const atRisk = persons.filter(p => p.average_wellbeing < 50).length;

  document.getElementById('kpiPersons').textContent = stats.total_unique_persons;
  document.getElementById('kpiPersonsMeta').textContent = 'Across ' + (stats.total_schools || 1) + ' school(s), ' + stats.total_dates_analyzed + ' day(s)';
  document.getElementById('kpiWell').innerHTML = avgW + '<span class="kpi-unit">%</span>';
  document.getElementById('kpiWellMeta').innerHTML = '<span class="' + (avgW >= 70 ? 'kpi-good' : avgW >= 50 ? 'kpi-warn' : 'kpi-bad') + '">' + (avgW >= 70 ? '↑ Good' : avgW >= 50 ? '→ Moderate' : '↓ Needs attention') + '</span>';
  document.getElementById('kpiEng').innerHTML = avgE + '<span class="kpi-unit">%</span>';
  document.getElementById('kpiDays').textContent = stats.total_dates_analyzed;
  document.getElementById('kpiDaysMeta').textContent = (stats.date_range[0] || '') + ' → ' + (stats.date_range[stats.date_range.length - 1] || '');

  const extraKpi = document.getElementById('kpiAtRisk');
  if (extraKpi) {
    extraKpi.textContent = atRisk;
    document.getElementById('kpiAtRiskMeta').textContent = 'Wellbeing below 50%';
  }
}

function renderTopPersons(report) {
  const allPersons = Object.values(report.person_profiles);
  const schools = report.overall_stats.schools || [];

  let html = '';

  if (schools.length > 0) {
    schools.forEach(school => {
      const schoolPersons = allPersons.filter(p => p.school === school);
      if (!schoolPersons.length) return;

      const avgW = Math.round(schoolPersons.reduce((a,p) => a + p.average_wellbeing, 0) / schoolPersons.length);
      const atRisk = schoolPersons.filter(p => p.average_wellbeing < 50).sort((a,b) => a.average_wellbeing - b.average_wellbeing);
      const topDetected = [...schoolPersons].sort((a,b) => b.total_detections - a.total_detections).slice(0, 4);

      const schoolColor = avgW >= 70 ? 'var(--green)' : avgW >= 50 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--red)';

      html += `<div style="background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;margin-bottom:24px;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="padding:20px 24px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;background:var(--bg)">
          <div>
            <div style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif;font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--text-primary)">${school}</div>
            <div style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:2px">${schoolPersons.length} unique persons · ${atRisk.length} need monitoring</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align:right">
            <div style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif;font-size:1.75rem;font-weight:800;color:${schoolColor}">${avgW}%</div>
            <div style="font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text-muted);font-weight:600;letter-spacing:0.05em">AVG WELLBEING</div>
          </div>
        </div>`;

      if (atRisk.length > 0) {
        html += `<div style="padding:16px 24px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);background:#fff8f8">
          <div style="font-size:0.7rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--red);letter-spacing:0.07em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-bottom:10px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">
            <svg width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><path d="M10.29 3.86L1.82 18a2 2 0 001.71 3h16.94a2 2 0 001.71-3L13.71 3.86a2 2 0 00-3.42 0z"/><line x1="12" y1="9" x2="12" y2="13"/><line x1="12" y1="17" x2="12.01" y2="17"/></svg>
            Needs Monitoring
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">`;
        atRisk.slice(0, 5).forEach(p => {
          const lowestTrait = p.avg_traits ? Object.entries(p.avg_traits).sort((a,b) => a[1]-b[1])[0] : null;
          const traitLabel = lowestTrait ? lowestTrait[0].replace(/_/g,' ') : '';
          html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:10px 12px;background:white;border:1px solid #fca5a5;border-radius:8px">
            <img src="data:image/jpeg;base64,${p.profile_image}" style="width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:6px;object-fit:cover;border:1px solid #fca5a5;flex-shrink:0">
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
              <div style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.875rem;color:var(--text-primary);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">${p.name}</div>
              <div style="font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-muted)">Lowest: ${traitLabel} (${lowestTrait ? lowestTrait[1] : '—'}%)</div>
            </div>
            <div style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif;font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--red);min-width:36px;text-align:right">${p.average_wellbeing}%</div>
          </div>`;
        });
        html += `</div></div>`;
      }

      html += `<div style="padding:16px 24px">
        <div style="font-size:0.7rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--text-muted);letter-spacing:0.07em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-bottom:10px">Top Tracked — Wellbeing Over Time</div>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:14px" id="topCards_${school.replace(/\s/g,'_')}">`;
      topDetected.forEach((p,pi) => {
        const wc = p.average_wellbeing >= 70 ? '#10b981' : p.average_wellbeing >= 50 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
        const cid = 'mini_' + p.person_id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'_');
        html += `<div style="background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;padding:12px 14px">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin-bottom:10px">
            <img src="data:image/jpeg;base64,${p.profile_image}" style="width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:6px;object-fit:cover;border:1px solid var(--border);flex-shrink:0">
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
              <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:0.875rem;color:var(--text-primary)">${p.name}</div>
              <div style="font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-muted)">${p.total_detections} detections · ${p.days_present} days · <span style="color:${wc};font-weight:700">${p.average_wellbeing}% avg</span></div>
            </div>
            <button onclick="goToPersonTemporal('${p.person_id}')" style="font-size:0.7rem;padding:4px 10px;border:1px solid var(--accent);border-radius:6px;background:var(--accent-light);color:var(--accent);cursor:pointer;font-weight:600">Full chart →</button>
          </div>
          <div style="position:relative;height:70px"><canvas id="${cid}"></canvas></div>
          <div style="font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:4px;text-align:center">Wellbeing score over time (day avg)</div>
        </div>`;
      });
      html += `</div></div></div>`;
    });
  } else {
    const top = allPersons.sort((a,b) => b.total_detections - a.total_detections).slice(0, 6);
    html = '<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px">';
    top.forEach(p => {
      const wc = p.average_wellbeing >= 70 ? '#10b981' : p.average_wellbeing >= 50 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
      html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px">
        <img src="data:image/jpeg;base64,${p.profile_image}" style="width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:8px;object-fit:cover">
        <div style="flex:1"><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.875rem">${p.name}</div><div style="font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-muted)">${p.school || ''} · ${p.total_detections} detections</div></div>
        <div style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif;font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:800;color:${wc}">${p.average_wellbeing}%</div>
      </div>`;
    });
    html += '</div>';
  }

  document.getElementById('topPersonsList').innerHTML = html || '<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">👁️</div><div class="empty-title">No data yet</div></div>';

  // Build mini sparkline charts after DOM is set
  if (schools.length > 0) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      schools.forEach(school => {
        const schoolPersons = allPersons.filter(p => p.school === school);
        const topDetected = [...schoolPersons].sort((a,b) => b.total_detections - a.total_detections).slice(0, 4);
        topDetected.forEach(p => {
          const cid = 'mini_' + p.person_id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'_');
          if (p.temporal_series && p.temporal_series.length) {
            buildMiniChart(cid, p.temporal_series, 'wellbeing');
          }
        });
      });
    }, 100);
  }
}

function goToPersonTemporal(pid) {
  const btn = document.querySelector('.nav-link:nth-child(4)');
  goTo('temporal', btn);
  setTimeout(() => {
    const sel = document.getElementById('temporalPersonSelect');
    if (sel) { sel.value = pid; renderTemporalCharts(); }
  }, 150);
}

function renderProfiles(report) {
  const persons = Object.values(report.person_profiles).sort((a,b) => b.total_detections - a.total_detections);
  if (!persons.length) {
    document.getElementById('profilesGrid').innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">👤</div><div class="empty-title">No profiles</div></div>';
    return;
  }

  const TRAIT_LABELS = {
    emotional_positivity: 'Emotional Positivity',
    stress_resilience: 'Stress Resilience',
    social_engagement: 'Social Engagement',
    social_confidence: 'Social Confidence',
    physical_energy: 'Physical Energy',
    posture_health: 'Posture Health',
    body_openness: 'Body Openness',
    focus_alertness: 'Focus & Alertness',
    facial_relaxation: 'Facial Relaxation',
    vitality_glow: 'Vitality & Glow'
  };

  const TRAIT_ICONS = {
    emotional_positivity: '😊',
    stress_resilience: '🛡️',
    social_engagement: '🤝',
    social_confidence: '💬',
    physical_energy: '⚡',
    posture_health: '🧍',
    body_openness: '🙌',
    focus_alertness: '👁️',
    facial_relaxation: '😌',
    vitality_glow: '✨'
  };

  const schools = [...new Set(persons.map(p => p.school || 'Unknown'))].sort();
  let html = '';
  schools.forEach(school => {
    const schoolPersons = persons.filter(p => (p.school || 'Unknown') === school);
    const avgW = Math.round(schoolPersons.reduce((a,p) => a + p.average_wellbeing, 0) / schoolPersons.length);
    const schoolColor = avgW >= 70 ? 'var(--green)' : avgW >= 50 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--red)';
    html += `<div style="margin-bottom:40px">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:16px;padding-bottom:12px;border-bottom:2px solid var(--border)">
        <div style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif;font-size:1.2rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--text-primary)">${school}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:16px">
          <span style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--text-muted)">${schoolPersons.length} persons</span>
          <span style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif;font-size:1rem;font-weight:800;color:${schoolColor}">${avgW}% avg wellbeing</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="person-grid">`;

    schoolPersons.forEach(p => {
    const wc = p.average_wellbeing >= 70 ? 'well-high' : p.average_wellbeing >= 50 ? 'well-mid' : 'well-low';
    const cc = p.average_wellbeing < 40 ? 'flagged' : p.average_wellbeing < 60 ? 'warn' : '';
    const engTag = p.average_engagement >= 60 ? 'tag-green' : p.average_engagement >= 40 ? 'tag-amber' : 'tag-red';
    const wbColor = p.average_wellbeing >= 70 ? 'var(--green)' : p.average_wellbeing >= 50 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--red)';
    const traits = p.avg_traits || {};

    let traitsHtml = '';
    Object.keys(TRAIT_LABELS).forEach(tk => {
      const val = traits[tk] !== undefined ? traits[tk] : 50;
      const barColor = val >= 70 ? '#10b981' : val >= 45 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
      traitsHtml += `
        <div style="margin-bottom:7px">
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:3px">
            <span style="font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-secondary);font-weight:500">${TRAIT_ICONS[tk]} ${TRAIT_LABELS[tk]}</span>
            <span style="font-size:0.72rem;font-weight:700;color:${barColor}">${val}%</span>
          </div>
          <div style="width:100%;height:4px;background:var(--surface2);border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden">
            <div style="width:${val}%;height:100%;background:${barColor};border-radius:2px;transition:width 0.5s ease"></div>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    });

    html += `<div class="person-card ${cc}" style="padding:0;overflow:hidden">
      <!-- Portrait photo area — full body crop shown large -->
      <div style="position:relative;width:100%;background:var(--surface2)">
        <img src="data:image/jpeg;base64,${p.profile_image}" id="avatar-${p.person_id}"
          style="width:100%;height:260px;object-fit:cover;object-position:top center;display:block;image-rendering:-webkit-optimize-contrast">
        <!-- Upload photo button -->
        <label title="Replace photo" style="position:absolute;top:10px;right:42px;width:30px;height:30px;background:rgba(37,99,235,0.88);border-radius:8px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;cursor:pointer;border:2px solid white;backdrop-filter:blur(4px)">
          <svg width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width="2.5"><path d="M21 15v4a2 2 0 01-2 2H5a2 2 0 01-2-2v-4"/><polyline points="17 8 12 3 7 8"/><line x1="12" y1="3" x2="12" y2="15"/></svg>
          <input type="file" accept="image/*" style="display:none" onchange="uploadPhoto(event,'${p.person_id}')">
        </label>
        <!-- Delete button -->
        <button onclick="deletePerson('${p.person_id}','${p.name.replace(/'/g,"&#39;")}')"
          title="Delete profile"
          style="position:absolute;top:10px;right:10px;width:30px;height:30px;background:rgba(220,38,38,0.88);border-radius:8px;border:2px solid white;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;backdrop-filter:blur(4px)">
          <svg width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width="2.5"><polyline points="3 6 5 6 21 6"/><path d="M19 6l-1 14H6L5 6"/><path d="M10 11v6M14 11v6"/><path d="M9 6V4h6v2"/></svg>
        </button>
        <!-- Wellbeing badge overlay -->
        <div style="position:absolute;bottom:10px;left:10px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.92);border-radius:8px;padding:4px 10px;backdrop-filter:blur(4px)">
          <span style="font-family:'Syne',sans-serif;font-size:1.3rem;font-weight:800;color:${wbColor}">${p.average_wellbeing}%</span>
          <span style="font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text-muted);font-weight:600;margin-left:3px">WELLBEING</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <!-- Info section below photo -->
      <div style="padding:14px 16px">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px">
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div class="person-name" onclick="openModal('${p.person_id}','${p.name.replace(/'/g,"&#39;")}')" style="font-size:1rem;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer">
              ${p.name}
              <svg style="display:inline;vertical-align:middle;margin-left:4px" width="11" height="11" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><path d="M11 4H4a2 2 0 00-2 2v14a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2v-7"/><path d="M18.5 2.5a2.121 2.121 0 013 3L12 15l-4 1 1-4 9.5-9.5z"/></svg>
            </div>
            <div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:2px">${p.person_id}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="person-tags" style="margin-bottom:10px">
          <span class="tag tag-blue">${p.days_present}d tracked</span>
          <span class="tag ${engTag}">${p.average_engagement}% eng</span>
          <span class="tag tag-blue">${p.total_detections} detections</span>
        </div>
        <div class="well-track"><div class="well-fill ${wc}" style="width:${p.average_wellbeing}%"></div></div>
        <div style="margin-top:12px">
          <div style="font-size:0.68rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--text-muted);letter-spacing:0.07em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-bottom:8px">10-Trait Behavioral Analysis</div>
          ${traitsHtml}
        </div>
        <div class="metrics-list" style="margin-top:10px">
          <div class="metric-item"><span class="metric-k">First Seen</span><span class="metric-v">${p.dates_seen[0] || '—'}</span></div>
          <div class="metric-item"><span class="metric-k">Last Seen</span><span class="metric-v">${p.dates_seen[p.dates_seen.length-1] || '—'}</span></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
    });
    html += `</div></div>`;
  });
  document.getElementById('profilesGrid').innerHTML = html;
}

function renderDaily(report) {
  const dates = report.overall_stats.date_range;
  if (!dates.length) return;
  let strip = '';
  dates.forEach((d, i) => {
    strip += `<div class="date-pill ${i===0?'active':''}" onclick="selectDate('${d}',this)">${d}</div>`;
  });
  document.getElementById('dateStrip').innerHTML = strip;
  showDate(dates[0], report);
}

function selectDate(date, el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.date-pill').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  el.classList.add('active');
  showDate(date, currentData);
}

function showDate(date, report) {
  const dd = report.date_summaries[date];
  if (!dd) return;
  const html = `<div class="kpi-row">
    <div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Unique Persons</div><div class="kpi-num">${dd.summary.unique_persons}</div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Avg Wellbeing</div><div class="kpi-num">${dd.summary.average_wellbeing}<span class="kpi-unit">%</span></div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Total Detections</div><div class="kpi-num">${dd.summary.total_detections}</div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card"><div class="kpi-label">Videos</div><div class="kpi-num">${dd.videos.length}</div></div>
  </div>
  <div class="content-card">
    <div class="content-card-header"><div class="content-card-title">Videos Analyzed on ${date}</div></div>
    <div class="content-card-body">
      ${dd.videos.map(v => `<div style="padding:12px 16px;background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center"><span style="font-weight:600;font-size:0.875rem">${v.video_name}</span><span style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--text-muted)">${v.total_frames_analyzed} frames analyzed</span></div>`).join('')}
    </div>
  </div>`;
  document.getElementById('dailyContent').innerHTML = html;
}

function renderTimeline(report) {
  const persons = Object.values(report.person_profiles).sort((a,b) => b.total_detections - a.total_detections);
  if (!persons.length) return;

  let html = '<div class="timeline-stream">';
  persons.forEach(p => {
    p.dates_seen.forEach((date, i) => {
      const isLast = i === p.dates_seen.length - 1;
      html += `<div class="tl-item">
        <div class="tl-spine"><div class="tl-dot"></div>${!isLast ? '<div class="tl-line"></div>' : ''}</div>
        <div class="tl-body">
          <div class="tl-date">${date}</div>
          <div class="tl-person">${p.name}</div>
          <div class="tl-meta">${p.school || ''} · ${p.person_id} · Wellbeing ${p.average_wellbeing}% · Engagement ${p.average_engagement}%</div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    });
  });
  html += '</div>';
  document.getElementById('timelineContent').innerHTML = html;
}

async function deletePerson(personId, personName) {
  if (!confirm('Delete profile for "' + personName + '"?\nThis cannot be undone.')) return;
  try {
    const resp = await fetch('/delete_person', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ person_id: personId })
    });
    const data = await resp.json();
    if (data.success) {
      if (currentData && currentData.person_profiles) {
        delete currentData.person_profiles[personId];
        renderAll(currentData);
      }
    } else {
      alert('Error: ' + data.message);
    }
  } catch(e) { console.error('Delete error:', e); }
}

async function uploadPhoto(event, personId) {
  const file = event.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = async function(e) {
    const dataUrl = e.target.result;
    const b64 = dataUrl.split(',')[1];
    try {
      const resp = await fetch('/update_person_photo', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ person_id: personId, image_b64: b64 })
      });
      const data = await resp.json();
      if (data.success) {
        const img = document.getElementById('avatar-' + personId);
        if (img) img.src = dataUrl;
        if (currentData.person_profiles && currentData.person_profiles[personId]) {
          currentData.person_profiles[personId].profile_image = b64;
        }
      }
    } catch(e) { console.error('Photo upload error:', e); }
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function openModal(personId, currentName) {
  editingPersonId = personId;
  document.getElementById('modalNameInput').value = currentName;
  document.getElementById('editModal').classList.add('active');
  setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('modalNameInput').focus(), 100);
}

function closeModal() {
  document.getElementById('editModal').classList.remove('active');
  editingPersonId = null;
}

async function saveModalName() {
  const newName = document.getElementById('modalNameInput').value.trim();
  if (!newName || !editingPersonId) return;
  try {
    const resp = await fetch('/update_person_name', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ person_id: editingPersonId, name: newName })
    });
    const data = await resp.json();
    if (data.success) {
      if (currentData.person_profiles && currentData.person_profiles[editingPersonId]) {
        currentData.person_profiles[editingPersonId].name = newName;
        renderAll(currentData);
      }
      closeModal();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Error updating name:', e);
  }
}

document.getElementById('editModal').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  if (e.target === this) closeModal();
});

document.getElementById('modalNameInput').addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
  if (e.key === 'Enter') saveModalName();
  if (e.key === 'Escape') closeModal();
});

async function loadSystemInfo() {
  try {
    const resp = await fetch('/system_info');
    const data = await resp.json();
    document.getElementById('settingDeepface').textContent = data.deepface ? '✓ Available' : '✗ Not installed';
    document.getElementById('settingMediapipe').textContent = data.mediapipe ? '✓ Available' : '✗ Not installed';
    document.getElementById('settingFaceRec').textContent = data.face_recognition ? '✓ Available' : '✗ Not installed';
  } catch (e) {}
}

async function loadExistingData() {
  try {
    const resp = await fetch('/get_report');
    const data = await resp.json();
    if (data.success && data.report) {
      currentData = data.report;
      renderAll(data.report);
      document.getElementById('dashDateSub').textContent = 'Loaded from saved report';
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

loadSystemInfo();
loadExistingData();
</script>
</body>
